- 7239 files · ~8,983,172 words

## Summary
- 56219 nodes · 115899 edges · 4175 communities (2415 shown, 1760 thin omitted)

- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `61335ee4`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
